# Magic grammar: glyph table and every permutation

GENERATED by `python scripts/magic_grammar.py` from the rules in
`docs/PLAN_magic_grammar.md`. Do not hand-edit; change the script (the rules)
or the glyph table in it and regenerate. Every row below is *derived* from the
same six parse rules, so a row that reads wrong is a rule that is wrong, not a
row to patch.

Notation: `⋈` transmute (A ⋈ B); `◂` the operator took the word on its
left; `▸` on its right; `_` a required word that was missing (the operator
fizzles, charged); `×N` a merged run; `‖` a clause boundary (a second
delivery); **bold** is the clause's delivery, `hand` when none was spoken.
Cost shape: `N×[payload]·carry(delivery)` — N instances, each paying the
payload tariff, times the delivery's carry premium, plus the word costs.

## 1. The glyph table (44 glyphs)

Sorts: **matter** names a material; **effect** happens at a point; **delivery**
decides where and when; **mod** edits the delivery record; **operator** takes
words on its declared side(s) and produces one of the others. `repeat`: `add`
= saying it again adds one more of its axis (fire×2 throws twice the voxels,
explosive×2 is twice the power); `compose` = applying the mod again
(shotgun×2 is 3·3 = 9, swift×2 is ×4, float×2 is gravity reversed).

| glyph | sort | word | repeat | axis | takes | says |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `air` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 0 | Nothing. The word for empty space. Sprayed it does nothing; as a target it unmakes; as a source it conjures. |
| `water` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 1 | The word for water. |
| `sand` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 1 | Loose grains. The cheapest matter there is. |
| `dirt` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 1 | Soil. |
| `stone` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 2 | Rock. |
| `wood` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 2 | Timber. Burns. |
| `fire` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 3 | Flame. Sprayed it ignites what it lands on. |
| `blood` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 3 | Blood. The cheapest thing a body can be mended with. |
| `flesh` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 4 | Meat. |
| `bone` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 5 | Bone. |
| `acid` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 6 | Eats stone and meat alike. |
| `lava` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 8 | Molten stone. |
| `steel` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 12 | Does not burn, does not dissolve. Dear. |
| `gold` | matter | 1 | add | voxels (spray) / volume share | value 40 | The dearest common matter. Making it is what kills alchemists. |
| `anything` | matter | 2 | add | voxels | value actual × surcharge | The wildcard. Matches whatever is actually there. DANGEROUS: it is priced as the matter it turns out to be, plus a surcharge, and billed when it resolves - you do not know the price until it lands. The surcharge is a knob (anythingSurchargeMille, +50% to start). |
| `explosive` | effect | 6 | add | power |  | An explosion at the point. Power adds with repetition; radius follows the engine law; priced by volume. |
| `gust` | effect | 5 | add | speed |  | A wind jet from the point along the aim. Repetition doubles speed, not size. |
| `implode` | effect | 5 | add | speed |  | A vacuum burst: pulls loose matter and bodies toward the point. |
| `spark` | effect | 2 | add | embers |  | One ember. Ignites a flammable cell it touches, nothing more. The cheap way to light things. |
| `transmute` | operator | 4 | add | volume | M ◂ transmute ▸ M → E | A transmute B: turns A into B where it resolves. Needs BOTH words; an empty side fizzles (charged). |
| `mend` | operator | 6 | add | voxels per tick | M ◂ mend → E | M mend: draws matter M from the resolve point into the caster's missing anatomy cells, one voxel at a time. Needs a source word. |
| `trail` | operator | 8 | add | budget | E/M ◂ trail → X | E trail: runs E at every marked voxel of the flight path. Needs a left word (matter is sprayed). |
| `aura` | operator | 7 | add | attach radius | E/M/X ◂ aura → E | E aura: SUSTAINS E (or a mod) on the body at the resolve point - or on the place, if no body is there. Runs every tick, billed per tick to the caster and reserved out of their max mana, until the caster drops it or runs dry. `aura self` is a ward on yourself; `aura projectile` puts the same thing on whoever the bolt hits. |
| `null` | operator | 5 | add | radius | any ◂ null → E | W null: a filter that refuses incoming ops of W's kind within the radius. Takes the word before it raw. |
| `echo` | operator | 6 | add | repeats | E/M ◂ echo → E | E echo: E repeats at the point every few ticks for a bounded span. |
| `projectile` | delivery | 6 | add | weight (speed, life, kinetic impact) | flight, carry 3.0× | A bolt from the hand. Medium speed, resolves on impact. |
| `bolt` | delivery | 8 | add | weight (speed, life, kinetic impact) | flight, carry 4.0× | Fast and light: twice the speed, half the kinetic impact. |
| `lob` | delivery | 4 | add | weight (speed, life, kinetic impact) | flight, carry 2.0× | Slow, arcing, heavy. Resolves on impact. |
| `orb` | delivery | 7 | add | weight (speed, life, kinetic impact) | flight, carry 2.5× | Slow, weightless, lingers; resolves on impact or when its life runs out. |
| `bomb` | delivery | 3 | add | weight (speed, life, kinetic impact) | flight, carry 1.0× | A dropped rigid body with a fuse. Resolves where it comes to rest when the fuse runs out. The cheapest carrier. |
| `beam` | delivery | 5 | add | weight (speed, life, kinetic impact) | continuous, carry 1.5× | Held: resolves at the ray hit every tick, billed per tick. |
| `self` | delivery | 2 | add | weight (speed, life, kinetic impact) | instant, carry 1.0× | At the caster. From the character screen, at the chosen body part. |
| `shotgun` | mod | 5 | compose | count x3 (3, 9, 27 ...), fanned | count x3 (3, 9, 27 ...), fanned | Three of it, fanned. Said again: nine. Everything behind it is paid that many times. |
| `split` | mod | 6 | compose | children x2 on resolve, gen+1 | children x2 on resolve, gen+1 | On resolve, two children with the same payload. Generation-capped. |
| `float` | mod | 4 | compose | gravity -1g | gravity -1g | Anti-gravity. Twice: gravity reversed. On a body-anchored delivery it lifts the body. |
| `heavy` | mod | 3 | compose | gravity +1g | gravity +1g | Falls harder, hits harder. |
| `swift` | mod | 4 | compose | speed x2 | speed x2 | Faster. |
| `slow` | mod | 2 | compose | speed /2 | speed /2 | Slower. A slow explosive bolt is a mine that drifts. |
| `long` | mod | 3 | compose | life x2 | life x2 | Lives longer, reaches further; a bomb's fuse is longer. |
| `wide` | mod | 5 | compose | resolve radius x2 | resolve radius x2 | Everything resolves over a wider radius. Priced by volume, so x8 per word. |
| `bounce` | mod | 4 | compose | bounces +1 | bounces +1 | Reflects off what it hits once more before resolving. |
| `pierce` | mod | 5 | compose | pierce +1 | pierce +1 | Passes through one body or thin wall before resolving. |
| `seek` | mod | 8 | compose | homing +1 | homing +1 | Turns toward the nearest body. |
| `fuse` | mod | 3 | compose | delay +30 ticks | delay +30 ticks | Waits after impact before resolving; on a bomb, a longer fuse. |

Rules the table relies on (from the plan): an operator with a missing required
word is **incomplete** — it is charged and does nothing (`transmute` needs
both `A` and `B`; `anything` and `air` are real words for the wildcard and the
void). `anything` is priced as the conversion it turns out to be PLUS the
actual matter's value times a surcharge (a knob, +50% to start), billed when it
resolves. A mod on a body-anchored delivery (`hand`, `self`, `beam`) acts
ONCE on the caster's body where that means anything (`float self` hops you up)
and is otherwise a charged no-op (`bounce self`). `aura` is the one way an
effect or a mod becomes SUSTAINED: `E aura` takes the word before it and
attaches it to the body at the resolve point (or the place, if no body), every
tick, billed per tick to the caster until dropped or dry. So `float aura self`
is a floaty ward on yourself and `float aura projectile` puts the same thing on
whoever the bolt hits. Every unary operator takes the word BEFORE it
(`fire trail`, `blood mend`, `float aura`, `transmute null`, `explosive echo`);
only `transmute` is infix.

## 4. Every ordered pair over a 19-word alphabet (308 distinct casts from 361 sequences)

Alphabet: `fire`, `gold`, `air`, `anything`, `explosive`, `gust`, `transmute`, `mend`, `trail`, `null`, `aura`, `echo`, `shotgun`, `float`, `split`, `projectile`, `bomb`, `self`, `beam`. Sequences that lower to the
same cast (R6: bag order is irrelevant) are listed once with their other
spellings.

| spoken | parse | what happens | cost shape |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fire fire` | fire×2 | from the hand, at reach — throws 6 voxels of fire | [fire×2]·carry(hand 1.0) + words 2 |
| `fire gold` (+1 same: `gold fire`) | fire gold | from the hand, at reach — throws 3 voxels of fire; throws 3 voxels of gold | [fire + gold]·carry(hand 1.0) + words 2 |
